Let f=1+r cos(ω t + φ), ω=2π. For 0≤x≤1:
g(x) = x + r * L(x) + r^2 * Q(x),
where L = (sin(ωx+φ) - sinφ)/ω? Wait sum of two integrals = 2( -cos? ). General integral I1=∫_0^x r cos(ωt+φ) dt = r( sin(ωx+φ)-sinφ)/ω.
I2=∫_0^x r cos(ω(x-t)+φ) dt = substitute u=x-t: ∫_x^0 r cos(ωu+φ)(-du)=∫_0^x r cos(ωu+φ)du = same. So linear total L_total = 2r( sin(ωx+φ)-sinφ)/ω = (r/π)(sin(ωx+φ)-sinφ). Correct.
Q: ∫_0^x cos(ωt+φ) cos(ω(x-t)+φ) dt.
Use cosAcosB=1/2[cos(A-B)+cos(A+B)]. A=ωt+φ, B=ωx-ωt+φ.
A-B=2ωt -ωx. (φ cancels). A+B=ωx+2φ.
∫ cos(2ωt-ωx) dt = [sin(2ωt-ωx)/(2ω)]_0^x = (sin(ωx)-sin(-ωx))/(2ω)= sin(ωx)/ω.
∫ cos(A+B) dt = x cos(ωx+2φ).
Thus Q_total = 1/2 [sin(ωx)/ω + x cos(ωx+2φ)].
So g(x)=x + (r/π)(sin(2πx+φ)-sinφ) + (r^2/2)[ sin(2πx)/(2π) + x cos(2πx+2φ) ].
Yes. This is for 0≤x≤1. By symmetry g(2-x)=g(x). Max over [0,2] same as [0,1]. Good.

Generic nonlinear local search idea:
We have p (sum1). Want minimize max c. Use **minimax smoothising**:
F_β(p) = β^{-1} logsumexp(β c). We can perform **Frank-Wolfe** or **mirror descent** in log p:
Let y = log p. p=softmax(y). Objective f(y)=J(softmax(y)). Since softmax is invariant to adding constant, we can project by subtracting mean. Gradient: ∇_y J = (∂c/∂p)^T ∂J/∂c * p (because dp_i/dy_i = p_i(δ_ii - p_i), but if ignore -p_i p term? Since functions are homogeneous degree 2 in p and sum constraint, maybe simplify. If J is max of c_k (homogeneous degree 2), along softmax, gradient can be approximated. We can instead optimize p directly with simplex constraint using projected gradient: p_new = projection_{simplex}(p - η g), where g_i = p_i + p_{k-i} for active lag? Derivative of c_k at p: for k, d c_k/dp_i = p_{k-i} if i+k? counted. More accurately: (2 Q_k p)_i with special diagonal. We can compute gradients for all lags efficiently by convolution of p with an "indicator"? Actually gradient of c_k: (C p)_i where C is symmetric matrix with C_{i,j}=1 if i+j=k? For derivative of ∑_i p_i p_{k-i}, as ordered pairs, derivative = 2 (p shifted) except diagonal. This can be computed by taking convolution matrix transpose. For active set K, gradient g_i ≈ 2∑_{k∈K} p_{k-i} (with diagonal correction). This is essentially cross-correlation of weights w_k with p. If active lags all max, choose weights uniformly or softmax weights.

- `fft_conv_real(a, P=None)`: pad length power of 2 ≥2N-1. Use `rfft`/`irfft` for speed. Since only convolution with itself: `F=np.fft.rfft(a,n=P); c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, n=P)[:2N-1]`. For gradient cross-correlation need full convolution of p and r; use `fft`/`ifft`. Could combine.

- `smooth_objective(y, N, beta)`: Compute p from y, c, logsumexp. Return smooth score. Also maybe cache? Not.
  - To avoid repeated normalization cost? okay.
- `smooth_jacobian(y, N, beta)`: Compute p, c, weights w, h via cross_corr, g_p=2h, dot=np.dot(g_p,p), gy=p*(g_p-dot). Return (smooth_val, gy). Define top-level `fun_smooth_J(y, N, beta)`:
```
y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
y = y - np.mean(y)
y = np.clip(y, -8, 8)
p = np.exp(y)
s = p.sum()
p /= s
c = fft_self_conv(p)
c = np.maximum(c,0)
m = c.max()
# logsumexp
z = beta*(c - m)
expz=np.exp(z)
tau=expz.sum()
val=(m + np.log(tau))/beta * 2*N? Wait logsumexp of c, score =2N * logsumexp(beta c)/beta. Since c sum? To compare to score, multiply 2N. Could just minimize logsumexp; scale N constant. But final max score. Use val = (2*N)*(m+log(tau))/beta.
w=expz/tau
# gradient c wrt p:
h = cross_positive(p,w) # h_i=∑ w_k p_{k-i}
gp = 2*h
gp_dot = np.dot(gp,p)
gy = p*(gp - gp_dot)
# gradient val wrt y = (2N)/beta * d logsumexp /dy. d logsumexp/dp_i = w_i? Wait logsumexp c derivative wrt c_k =w_k; derivative wrt p_i =∑ w_k d c_k/dp_i = gp_i. So d (logsumexp)/dy= p*(gp-dot). Thus val gradient = (2N)/beta * gy.
grad = (2*N)/beta * gy
return val, grad
```

- For sweep in range:
  - Choose order: maybe all indices. For memory/time, we can iterate all indices but skip tiny p? We'll do all for N<=4096. To save time, if N>4096, sample top 2N? not.
  - For each i:
    - pi = p[i]; scale = max(pi, S/N*0.5, 1e-12)
    - x_values = np.array factors * scale. But factor list fixed. Include pi itself? yes factor 1. If pi is 0, scale mean. If pi large, max x maybe 4*scale could exceed S; okay but perhaps too large; if x > 10*S? cap at `10*S/N?`? Since score scale invariant, making one coordinate 10sum could score huge. Factor 8 enough. Use factors `[0.0, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, 1.3, 1.6, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0]`. M=15. Maybe x=0 removes coordinate (interior zeros possible) but length n counts zeros; may improve or worsen. Include 0.
    - Compute shifted = zeros(L); valid k from max(0, 2i-(N-1)?) Wait p_{k-i} valid if 0≤k-i≤N-1 -> k∈[i, i+N-1]. So shifted[i:i+N] = p[0:N] (since for k=i+j, p_j = p[j]). Indeed if k-i=j, j=0..N-1; k from i to i+N-1. That's a simple slice! For coordinate i, `shifted = np.zeros(L); shifted[i:i+N] = p`. Nice. The coefficient for pair p_i with p_j at lag k=i+j is 2. For k=2i, j=i, add x. So base = 2*shifted; add_arr zeros L; if 2i < N, add_arr[2i]=1.
    - Y = x - pi. c_new = c + Y[:,None]*(base + add_arr*x[:,None])
    - maxc = c_new.max(axis=1) (roundoff maybe tiny negative; take max, if negative? c nonnegative; use clip c_new? We can compute max without clipping, but if negative due drift? c_old from FFT positive; updates exact positive; okay. For safety `maxc = np.maximum(c_new,0).max(axis=1)` but adding max after subtraction? Use `maxc = c_new.max(axis=1); maxc = np.maximum(maxc, 0.0)`.
    - Sn = S - pi + x
    - scores = 2N*maxc/(Sn*Sn)
    - Choose x with min score. If best_score < current_score*(1-1e-10) maybe accept. But sometimes no improvement due grid; we can accept if < current_score - tol. Set tol = 1e-12 or maybe 1e-9.     - If accept: update p[i]=x_best; update c using diff for x_best (we can use formula); S=Sn.   - End i. After sweep, recompute c=fft_conv(p) and S=p.sum(), normalize p to sum1 (scale p, c), score. If score improved, keep; if not, maybe revert? We can keep best p externally.
- Return best.

Potential issue: c update formula for all k: We used base 2*shifted and add x at 2i. It assumes current c includes ordered convolution and p old. Let's verify with N=3, i=1, p=[a,b,c]. old center k=2: a c + b^2 + c a =2ac+b^2. shifted slice k=1..3 = [a,b,c]. At k=2 shifted=b; base2b; add x at k=2. diff=(x-b)*(2b + x). New center =2a? Wait new p=[a,x,c]. center=2ac + x^2. diff new-old =x^2-b^2 + (2ac cancels) = (x-b)(x+b). Our formula: (x-b)*(2b + x) = (x-b)(x+b). good. For k=1: old 2ab, new 2ax diff2a(x-b). shifted k=1 a, base2a, no add. good. k=3 old2bc new2cx? Wait for k=3: pairs (0,3 invalid?) N=3 indices0..2; k=3: p1p2? ordered: i=1 j=2? c3=p1*p2+p2*p1=2bc; new=2xc; diff2c(x-b). shifted k=3 c, base2c. good.

def generate_starts(N, deadline):
  starts=[]
  add_uniform, mother_resample
  t = np.arange(N)/N
  # linear + sine combos
  for u in [-0.4,-0.2,0,0.2,0.4]:
    for amp in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]:
      for m in [1,2,3]:
        for phase in [0, π/2, π, 3π/2]:
          f=1+u*(2t-1)+amp*np.sin(2π*m*t+phase); clip; norm; if sum>0 add
  # random 4 modes
  for seed in range(100):
    g=zeros; for m in [1,2,3,4,5]: coeff random normal; g += coeff*cos(2πm t+phase)
    p=exp(g clip); norm; add
  # single high sine with amplitude=1
  ...
  # Evaluate all via fft_score, sort, keep top unique? Could deduplicate by score and maybe l2 distance.
```

Pseudo for coordinate_update:
```
def coordinate_descent(p, deadline, max_sweeps=5, relax_factor=1.0):
    p = np.clip(np.asarray(p,float),0,None)
    S=p.sum()
    if S<=0: p=zeros(N); return
    p=p/S; S=1
    c=fft_self_conv(p)
    score=2*N*c.max()
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score
    N=p.size; L=2*N-1; mean=1/N
    factors_rel=np.array([...])
    factors_abs=np.array([...])
    rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    sweep=0
    while time_left > 30 and sweep<max_sweeps:
       order = rng.permutation(N)
       S=1.0
       improved=False
       for i in order:
          pi=p[i]
          scale = max(pi, 0.2*mean)
          cands = np.concatenate((factors_rel*scale, factors_abs*mean))
          cands = np.unique(cands[(cands>=0) & (cands<=max(1.0, 0.2*S))])
          if cands.size <=1: continue
          shifted=np.zeros(L); shifted[i:i+N]=p
          base=2*shifted
          add=np.zeros(L)
          ki=2*i
          if ki<L: add[ki]=1.0
          Y = cands - pi
          # We can precompute base; for x add: M= cands.size
          # c_new = c[None,:] + Y[:,None]*(base + add[None,:]*cands[None,:])
          # To save memory, compute inner = base + add*cands[:,None]
          inner = base[None,:] + cands[:,None]*add[None,:]
          cnew = c[None,:] + Y[:,None]*inner
          maxc = cnew.max(axis=1)
          maxc = np.maximum(maxc, 0.0)
          Sn = 1.0 - pi + cands  # since S normalized before? Wait within sweep we don't renormalize after each update? We maintain p sum S maybe not 1. Use S variable.
```
Within sweep if we update p and S not normalize, S changes. The formula Sn = S - pi + x. base uses current p (which may sum S not 1). `shifted=p` current. So Sn=S - pi + cands. Good. At start S=1. After accepted, S=Sn_best. If S drifts, p sum =S. We can normalize if |S-1|>0.2: p/=S; c/=S^2; S=1.
But if p sum S not1, mean = S/N; factors_abs use mean.
For candidate cap use maybe `max(2*S, 1.0)`.
`cnew = c[None,:] + (cands-pi)[:,None]*(base + cands[:,None]*add)` where base=2*shifted. Add part: at k=2i, base already 2p_i. Need add x; formula diff=(x-pi)*(2p_j + δ_x). So inner =2*shifted + x*add. Correct.
Score for each candidate: `sc = 2*N*maxc/(Sn*Sn)`. If Sn very small, inf. Pick min where finite.
If best candidate score < current score - tol:
   update p[i]=best_x; S = best_Sn; c += (best_x-pi)*(base + best_x*add)
   current_score=best_score; improved True
   if current_score < best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=current_score
After many updates, if S <0.9 or >1.1 maybe normalize:
   if S>0 and (S<0.8 or S>1.2): c /= (S*S); p /= S; S=1.0; current_score=2*N*c.max()
At sweep end recompute c via FFT: p normalized sum1, c, score. If score < best_score update.
```
Note: When we update c using formulas with current S not1, if later normalize, c scaled. Good.

Could relative factors be multiplied by `max(pi, 0.2*mean)`; if pi=0, scale=0.2mean; factors up to 4scale=0.8mean, plus abs up to50mean. Good.

Use `np.unique` each coordinate costly. We can precompute relative factors and abs factors arrays; for each i, `cands = np.concatenate((factors_rel*scale, factors_abs*mean))`; filter `cands = cands[(cands>=0)&(cands<=cap)]`; maybe duplicates but not need unique. Duplicates increase slight cost. We can unique only if small. Use `np.unique` okay.

Candidate factors:
`factors_rel = np.array([0.0,0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9,1.0,1.1,1.3,1.7,2.5,4.0])` (13)

Let's write helper `fft_self_conv(a)`:
```
def fft_self_conv(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    size=1
    while size < 2*n-1: size <<=1
    f=np.fft.rfft(a,n=size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f,n=size)[:2*n-1]
    return c.real
```
For coordinate initial c many times, this is okay. We can cache size? Use global dict? Could just compute each time? To save, define `next_pow2(length)` global. `while` each call maybe overhead. Use `p2 = 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(2*n-1)))`? `int(np.ceil(np.log2(...)))`. Simpler loop.

In coordinate, we recompute c by FFT only at sweep start, not each candidate. Good.

def fun_smooth_J(y, N, beta):
   y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
   y = y - y.mean()
   y = np.clip(y, -10, 10)
   p=np.exp(y); p/=p.sum()
   c=fft_self_conv(p)
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   m=c.max()
   # if beta <1? use beta float
   shifted = beta*(c-m)
   # For beta large, exp underflow okay.
   expz=np.exp(shifted)
   s=expz.sum()
   if not finite: return 1e6, zeros
   val=2*N*(m + np.log(s))/beta
   w=expz/s
   # cross positive
   L=2*N-1
   P=1; while P<3*N: P<<=1? For c self P>=2N-1; for cross p*r length 3N-2, need P>=3N-1. We can compute P_cross.
   Fr = fft of r? We already have Fp from c? In fun, compute Fp for c. To avoid recompute, compute Fp once.
```
Let's implement inside:
```
size_c=1; while size_c<2*N-1: size_c*=2
Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,n=size_c)
c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,n=size_c)[:L].real
# For cross, need size_x >= N+L-1 =3N-2
size_x=1; while size_x<3*N-1: size_x*=2
# Could reuse Fp zeropad to size_x? If size_x > size_c, recompute. Simpler recompute rfft(p,size_x)

Fr=np.fft.rfft(r,n=size_x)
conv=np.fft.irfft(Fp2*Fr,n=size_x).real
h=conv[2*N-2 : N-2 : -1]
```

Now candidate generation details in code:
```

def resample_sequence(arr, N):
    arr=np.asarray(arr,float); arr=np.clip(arr,0,None); s=arr.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(N)/N
    old_len=arr.size
    if old_len==N: return arr/s
    x_old=np.arange(old_len)
    x_new=np.linspace(0, old_len-1, N)
    new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr/s)
    return new
```

Let's detail `fun_smooth_J` memory/time:
```
def fun_smooth_J(y, N, beta):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size != N or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
       return 1e100, np.zeros(N)
    y = y - np.mean(y)
    y = np.clip(y, -12.0, 12.0)
    p = np.exp(y)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return 1e100, zeros
    p /= s
    P = get_fft_len_smooth(N)
    L = 2*N-1
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=P)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, n=P)[:L].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = np.max(c)
    if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e100, zeros
    # logsumexp
    z = beta * (c - m)
    # If beta huge and z contains large? shifted <=0, okay
    expz = np.exp(z)
    ssum = expz.sum()
    if ssum <=0 or not finite:
        return 1e100, zeros
    val = (2.0*N) * (m + np.log(ssum)) / beta
    w = expz / ssum
    # cross correlation
    r = np.zeros(P, dtype=float)
    r[:L] = w[::-1]
    Fr = np.fft.rfft(r, n=P)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fr, n=P).real
    # slice h
    if N == 1:
       h = np.array([w[0]*p[0]?])
    else:
       h = conv[2*N-2 : N-2 : -1]
    gp = 2.0 * h
    dot = float(np.dot(gp, p))
    gy = p * (gp - dot)
    grad = (2.0*N) / beta * gy
    # If grad contains nan, zero
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=zeros
    return float(val), grad
```
Check cross slice for N=1: L=1, conv length? P target 3N-1=2? P=2. r[:1]=w0. p length1. conv q index? h0 =? w0*p0. conv[0]? Our slice `2*N-2=0 : N-2=-1 : -1` gives index0? In Python `0:-1:-1` empty. Need handle N=1 separately. But N large; still robust. For N=1, score? Uniform length1: p=[1], c=[1], score=2. Smooth not used. We can handle: `h = np.array([w[0]*p[0]])`? For coordinate gradient of c0=p0^2, w0=1, gp=2p0=2, dot=2, gy=0. Fine. Use if.

For N>1, slice: e.g. N=2 -> start 2, stop 0, step -1 -> indices 2,1. good. N=3 -> start4, stop1 -> 4,3,2. good.

But `conv` length P maybe zero padding; slice within. Good.

The gradient `gy` sum? Since gp dot p =? For log derivative, `sum gy = ∑ p_i gp_i - ∑ p_i^2 dot = dot - dot =0` (if dot scalar), so no drift. Good.

`minimize` may call with `y` as list? convert.

Potential issue: `fun_smooth_J` with `beta` small (100) val approximates average c, not max; gradient may move to lower average but uniform already. Starting nonuniform, maybe. Sequence of beta.

But coordinate itself uses deterministic; perturbations can escape. Add function `perturb_sequence(p, rng)`.

```
def perturb_sequence(p, rng, alpha=0.2):
    q=p.copy()
    n=p.size
    # multiply random blocks
    for _ in range(rng.integers(2,6)):
       width=max(1,n//100)
       start=rng.integers(0,n)
       stop=min(n,start+width+ int(width*rng.random()))
       q[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, alpha))
    # optional mirror
    if rng.random()<0.2: q=0.5*(q+q[::-1])
    q=np.clip(q,0,None); s=q.sum(); if s>0: q/=s
    return q
```
No lambda. Use in propose if time. For each perturbation, coordinate with small subdeadline (30s), compare.

   if len(chosen)>=10: break
return chosen

```python
"""
Search for short non-negative step functions with small
2 * n * max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
- Generate a diverse set of smooth initial densities at a reduced length.
- Improve them with exact one-coordinate line-search descent, where each
  coordinate update minimizes the objective over a small geometric grid.
- Optionally use a low-dimensional smooth log-sum-exp optimizer for escapes.
- Keep the best sequence found before the deadline.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None


_FLATTEN_CACHE = {}

_REL_FACTORS = np.array(
    [0.0, 0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9, 1.0, 1.1, 1.3, 1.7, 2.5, 4.0],
    dtype=np.float64,
)
_ABS_FACTORS = np.array(
    [0.0, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0],
    dtype=np.float64,
)

_REL_FACTORS_LARGE = np.array(
    [0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9, 1.0, 1.2, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0],
    dtype=np.float64,
)
_ABS_FACTORS_LARGE = np.array(
    [0.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0],
    dtype=np.float64,
)


def next_pow2_int(x: int) -> int:
    """Smallest power of two >= x, with x at least 1."""
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(float(x))))


def get_fft_len_self(n: int) -> int:
    """FFT length sufficient for full convolution of length n with itself."""
    key = ("self", n)
    val = _FLATTEN_CACHE.get(key, None)
    if val is None:
        val = next_pow2_int(2 * n - 1)
        _FLATTEN_CACHE[key] = val
    return val


def get_fft_len_smooth(n: int) -> int:
    """FFT length sufficient for self convolution and cross-correlation helper."""
    key = ("smooth", n)
    val = _FLATTEN_CACHE.get(key, None)
    if val is None:
        val = next_pow2_int(3 * n - 1)
        _FLATTEN_CACHE[key] = val
    return val


def fft_self_conv(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Full linear convolution a * a using FFT.  a must be 1D real."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    pad = get_fft_len_self(n)
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, n=pad)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, n=pad)
    return c[:2 * n - 1].real


def clip_finite_nonnegative(a) -> np.ndarray:
    """Coerce to a finite, 1D, non-negative ndarray."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return a


def normalize_sum1(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Clipped nonnegative normalization to sum 1.  Returns zeros if invalid."""
    a = clip_finite_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def score_fft(a) -> float:
    """Approximate evaluator score using FFT convolution.  Scale invariant."""
    a = clip_finite_nonnegative(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = fft_self_conv(a)
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def resample_sequence(a, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Linearly resample a non-negative sequence to length n and normalize."""
    a = clip_finite_nonnegative(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = a / s
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.arange(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(a.size - 1), n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = clip_finite_nonnegative(out)
    t = out.sum()
    if t > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(t):
        out /= t
    else:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out


def try_add_candidate(starts: list, p, n: int) -> None:
    """Normalize/score a candidate and append it if valid."""
    p = clip_finite_nonnegative(p)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if p.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return
    p = p / s
    if p.size != n:
        p = resample_sequence(p, n)
    sc = score_fft(p)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        starts.append((float(sc), p.copy()))


def random_block_perturb(p: np.ndarray, rng: np.random.Generator, alpha: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """Random multiplicative block perturbation, preserving nonnegativity."""
    q = p.copy()
    n = q.size
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_sum1(q)
    rng = np.random.Generator if isinstance(rng, type) else rng
    blocks = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
    for _ in range(blocks):
        width = max(1, int(n // 80))
        width += int(width * float(rng.random()))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - max(1, width))))
        stop = min(n, start + width)
        if stop > start:
            q[start:stop] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, alpha)))
    if float(rng.random()) < 0.25:
        q = 0.5 * (q + q[::-1])
    q = clip_finite_nonnegative(q)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(s):
        q /= s
    else:
        q = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return q


def generate_start_candidates(n: int, deadline: float, seed: int) -> list:
    """Create and rank diverse smooth starting vectors of length n."""
    starts = []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    # Uniform, and previous construction if available.
    try_add_candidate(starts, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
    try:
        mother = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        mother_arr = clip_finite_nonnegative(mother)
        if mother_arr.size:
            try_add_candidate(starts, resample_sequence(mother_arr, n), n)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Linear ramp family.
    for u in (-0.45, -0.30, -0.15, 0.0, 0.15, 0.30, 0.45):
        f = 1.0 + u * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
        try_add_candidate(starts, np.maximum(f, 0.0), n)

    # Single sinusoidal modulation.
    for m in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6):
        for amp in (0.25, 0.5, 0.7, 0.85, 0.95, 1.0):
            for phase in (0.0, np.pi / 3.0, 2.0 * np.pi / 3.0,
                          np.pi, 4.0 * np.pi / 3.0, 5.0 * np.pi / 3.0):
                f = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + phase)
                try_add_candidate(starts, np.maximum(f, 0.0), n)
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break

    # Low-order polynomial chaos.
    l1 = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    l2 = 6.0 * t * t - 6.0 * t + 1.0
    l3 = 20.0 * t * t * t - 30.0 * t * t + 12.0 * t - 1.0
    for a in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8):
        for b in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8):
            for cc in (-0.35, 0.0, 0.35):
                f = 1.0 + a * l1 + b * l2 + cc * l3
                try_add_candidate(starts, np.maximum(f, 0.0), n)

    # Random low-frequency exponential spectra.
    modes = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6)
    want_random = 110
    used = 0
    for _ in range(want_random):
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in modes:
            coeff = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            g += coeff * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + phase)
        g = np.clip(g, -3.5, 3.5)
        p = np.exp(g)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            try_add_candidate(starts, p, n)
            used += 1
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break

    # Random clipped affine spectra.
    for _ in range(80):
        f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in modes:
            coeff = float(rng.uniform(-0.55, 0.55))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            f += coeff * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + phase)
        try_add_candidate(starts, np.maximum(f, 0.0), n)
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break

    if not starts:
        return [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    scores = np.array([sc for sc, _p in starts], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    chosen = []
    for idx in order:
        p = starts[int(idx)][1]
        dup = False
        for q in chosen:
            if np.linalg.norm(p - q) < 0.18:
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            chosen.append(p)
        if len(chosen) >= 10:
            break
    return chosen


def coordinate_descent(p, deadline: float, max_sweeps: int, seed: int):
    """
    One-coordinate line-search descent.

    For coordinate i, all other coordinates are held fixed and the scale of p_i
    is searched over a small grid.  The objective is evaluated in closed form
    from the current convolution vector, so no full convolution is needed for
    each coordinate candidate.
    """
    p = normalize_sum1(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, float("inf")

    rng = np.random.Generator(seed) if isinstance(seed, int) else seed

    rel_factors = _REL_FACTORS_LARGE if n > 6000 else _REL_FACTORS
    abs_factors = _ABS_FACTORS_LARGE if n > 6000 else _ABS_FACTORS

    l_full = 2 * n - 1
    s = 1.0
    c = fft_self_conv(p)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cur_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        return p, float("inf")

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = cur_score
    no_improve = 0

    for sweep in range(int(max_sweeps)):
        if time.monotonic() > max(deadline - 1.0, time.monotonic() + 0.1):
            break

        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                break

            pi = float(p[i])
            mean = float(s) / float(n)
            scale = max(pi, 0.2 * mean, 1e-300)

            cands = np.concatenate((rel_factors * scale, abs_factors * mean))
            cap = max(0.2, 5.0 * pi, 0.5 * max(s, 1e-12))
            cands = cands[(cands >= 0.0) & (cands <= cap)]
            cands = np.append(cands, pi)
            cands = cands[np.isfinite(cands)]
            if cands.size == 0:
                continue
            cands = np.unique(cands)
            if cands.size <= 1:
                continue

            shifted = np.zeros(l_full, dtype=np.float64)
            shifted[i:i + n] = p
            shifted *= 2.0

            y = cands - pi
            cnew = c[None, :] + y[:, None] * shifted[None, :]

            ki = 2 * i
            if 0 <= ki < l_full:
                cnew[:, ki] = c[ki] + y * (cands + pi)

            maxc = np.maximum(cnew.max(axis=1), 0.0)
            sn = s - pi + cands
            valid = sn > 1e-12
            sc = np.full(cands.shape, float("inf"), dtype=np.float64)
            if np.any(valid):
                sc[valid] = (2.0 * float(n) * maxc[valid]) / (sn[valid] * sn[valid])
                sc[~np.isfinite(sc)] = float("inf")

            idx = int(np.argmin(sc))
            if not np.isfinite(sc[idx]):
                continue
            if sc[idx] >= cur_score - 1e-10:
                continue

            x = float(cands[idx])
            dy = x - pi
            diff = dy * shifted
            if 0 <= ki < l_full:
                diff[ki] = dy * (x + pi)

            p[i] = x
            c = c + diff
            s = float(sn[idx])
            cur_score = float(sc[idx])
            improved = True

            if s < 0.8 or s > 1.25:
                inv = 1.0 / s
                p *= inv
                c *= inv * inv
                s = 1.0
                m = float(np.max(c))
                if m < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
                    m = 0.0
                cur_score = float(2.0 * n * m)

        # Recompute exact FFT convolution at the end of each sweep.
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            p = p / s
            s = 1.0
        c = fft_self_conv(p)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        sweep_score = float(2.0 * n * np.max(c)) if c.size else float("inf")

        if np.isfinite(sweep_score) and sweep_score < best_score:
            best_score = sweep_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            cur_score = sweep_score

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 2:
                break

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def fun_smooth_pair(y, n: int, beta: float):
    """
    Smooth surrogate objective and gradient in softmax parameters.

    Returns (value, gradient).  The value is a log-sum-exp approximation to
    the true score.  The gradient is with respect to the unconstrained y.
    """
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.shape != (n,):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    y = np.clip(y, -12.0, 12.0)
    pw = np.exp(y)
    ss = float(pw.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = pw / ss

    pad = get_fft_len_smooth(n)
    l_full = 2 * n - 1
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=pad)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, n=pad)[:l_full].real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    z = beta * (c - m)
    expz = np.exp(z)
    zsum = float(expz.sum())
    if zsum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(zsum):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    val = float((2.0 * n) * (m + np.log(zsum)) / beta)
    w = expz / zsum

    r = np.zeros(pad, dtype=np.float64)
    r[:l_full] = w[::-1]
    fr = np.fft.rfft(r, n=pad)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fp * fr, n=pad).real

    if n == 1:
        h = np.array([float(w[0]) * float(p[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        h = conv[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1]

    gp = 2.0 * h
    dot = float(np.dot(gp, p))
    gy = p * (gp - dot)
    grad = (2.0 * n / beta) * gy

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad


def smooth_optimize(p, n: int, deadline: float):
    """Short run of smoothed log-sum-exp L-BFGS on softmax parameters."""
    if minimize is None or n == 0:
        return p, score_fft(p)

    p = normalize_sum1(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score_fft(p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best_p, float("inf")

    y = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-12))

    for beta, maxiter in ((50, 35), (400, 35), (3000, 25), (20000, 18)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                fun_smooth_pair,
                y,
                args=(n, beta),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "ftol": 1e-10,
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "maxfun": int(3 * maxiter),
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
            y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if y.shape == (n,) and np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
                y = y - float(np.mean(y))
                y = np.clip(y, -18.0, 18.0)
                q = np.exp(y)
                s = float(q.sum())
                if s > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(s):
                    q = q / s
                    sc = score_fft(q)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = float(sc)
                        best_p = q.copy()
                        y = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-12))

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point.  Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - 8.0)

    base_n = 4096 if budget >= 1200.0 else 2048
    gen_n = 2048

    rng = np.random.Generator(seed)

    # 1. Build diversified starts at a reduced length.
    raw_gen_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 120.0)
    raw_starts = generate_start_candidates(gen_n, raw_gen_deadline, seed + 1)
    if not raw_starts:
        raw_starts = [np.ones(gen_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(gen_n)]

    # 2. Optional smooth optimizer on the two best starts.
    if minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 240.0:
        for j in range(min(2, len(raw_starts))):
            raw = raw_starts[j]
            old_score = score_fft(raw)
            sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
            raw2, sc2 = smooth_optimize(raw, gen_n, sub_deadline)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and np.isfinite(old_score) and sc2 < old_score - 1e-4:
                raw_starts[j] = raw2
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
                break

    # 3. Coordinate descent on several full-length starts.
    starts_to_run = []
    for raw in raw_starts[:6]:
        starts_to_run.append(resample_sequence(raw, base_n))

    if not starts_to_run:
        starts_to_run.append(np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(base_n))

    best_p = np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(base_n)
    best_score = 2.0

    per_start = max(60.0, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 180.0) / max(1, len(starts_to_run)))
    for j, p0 in enumerate(starts_to_run):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
            break
        alloc = min(320.0, per_start * (1.8 if j == 0 else 1.0))
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        p1, sc1 = coordinate_descent(p0, sub_deadline, max_sweeps=3, seed=seed + 100 + j)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc1)
            best_p = p1.copy()
            print(f"[propose] start {j}: score {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    # 4. Random perturbations of the current best to escape local minima.
    extra = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0 and best_score > 1.505 and extra < 6:
        alpha = 0.22 + 0.05 * extra
        p_pert = random_block_perturb(best_p, rng, alpha)
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + min(120.0, 90.0 + 12.0 * extra))
        p1, sc1 = coordinate_descent(p_pert, sub_deadline, max_sweeps=2, seed=seed + 900 + extra)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc1)
            best_p = p1.copy()
            print(f"[propose] perturb {extra}: score {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
        extra += 1

    # 5. Optional upsampling by 2x, if clearly beneficial.
    if base_n <= 4096 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        big_n = 2 * base_n
        p_big = resample_sequence(best_p, big_n)
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 200.0)
        p_big, sc_big = coordinate_descent(p_big, sub_deadline, max_sweeps=1, seed=seed + 5000)
        if np.isfinite(sc_big) and sc_big < best_score - 0.004:
            best_score = float(sc_big)
            best_p = p_big.copy()
            print(f"[propose] upsample to {big_n}: score {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    # 6. Final sanitation.
    best_p = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64)
    best_p = np.where(np.isfinite(best_p), best_p, 0.0)
    best_p = np.clip(best_p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.01:
        out_n = 2048
        return [1.0 / out_n] * out_n

    best_p = best_p / s
    best_p = np.clip(best_p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = [float(x) for x in best_p.tolist()]

    # Non-fatal exact check, only if there is ample time.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        try:
            exact = evaluate_sequence(out)  # noqa: F821
            if np.isfinite(exact):
                print(f"[propose] final approximate score {best_score:.9f}, exact check {float(exact):.9f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass
    else:
        print(f"[propose] final approximate score {best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    return out
```
